{"forms":[{"form":"yo-yos","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"I bought a yo-yo from the toy store yesterday.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A toy consisting of a spheroidal or cylindrical spindle having a circular groove in which string is wound; it is used by holding the string in the fingers and reeling the spindle up and down by movements of the wrist."]},{"glosses":["A volatile market that moves up and down."],"topics":["business","finance"]},{"glosses":["Someone who vacillates."],"tags":["informal"]},{"glosses":["A dogfighting maneuver involving the attacker temporarily exchanging altitude for airspeed, or vice versa, in order to rapidly catch up with the defender or to prevent an overshoot."],"topics":["aeronautics","aerospace","aviation","business","engineering","government","military","natural-sciences","physical-sciences","politics","war"]},{"glosses":["A cloth rosette formed by gathering the outside edge of a circle of fabric in toward the centre using a running stitch."],"topics":["business","manufacturing","sewing","textiles"]},{"examples":[{"text":"It is hard to watch the management for very long and not conclude that the place is run by a bunch of yo-yos.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A foolish, annoying or incompetent person."],"tags":["informal"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈjəʊ.jəʊ/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ˈjoʊ.joʊ/","tags":["US"]},{"ipa":"/ˈjəʉ.jəʉ/","tags":["Australia"]},{"ipa":"/ˈjɐʉ.jɐʉ/","tags":["New-Zealand"]},{"ipa":"/ˈjo.jo/","tags":["Scotland"]},{"ipa":"/ˈjoː.joː/","tags":["Wales"]}],"upstream_line":298080,"upstream_offset":1489837569,"upstream_record_sha256":"64b95ea32ab7248584eec629b5df0db5ca0ebe2dad412027469360cd640614d9","word":"yo-yo"}
